1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the integration object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 441. The moduli space has dimension at most 26 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 441 + O(1/n^26). Summing over all modes and applying the integration convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
